		#SEL - Seleukids
		SEL = {
			own_control_core = {
				basra abadan abdas al_madar al_qatr suq_al_shuyukh zuwayr #Basra
				an_nil nasiriyah numaniyyah rusafa samawa #Al-Bata'ih
				wasit abdanan al_tib arkvaz bakusaya dehloran meymeh #Wasit
				baghdad badra balad_ruz baqubah dayr_aqul khalis #Baghdad
				samarra tikrit ukbara #Samarra
				kufa ayn_tamr hillah karbala #Al-Kufa
				al_sinn balad duhok qayyarah sinjar tal_afar #Mosul
				gilan_gharb eyvan ilam khanaqin mandali #Hulwan
				ahvaz dowraq hamidiyeh hoveyzeh mosharahat muhammerah #Ahvaz
				bam fahraj_narmashir narmashir rayen rigan sepeh #Bam
				ramhormoz bagh_malek khalafabad machul quban #Ramhormoz
				behbahan dehdasht dishmok dogonbadan hendijan suq #Behbahan
				bandar_deylam #Qala-i Safid
				deh_kord borujen samiram sisakht vanak yasuj #Borujen
				borujerd aligurdaz borborud dorud gerit sibak #Deh-e Kurd
